### CJK-LOG-20260718-003 — CJKV research object is a typed crosswalk, not a merged language

- **Recorded:** 2026-07-18 (time not recorded; retrospective backfill after directive).
- **Work unit / authority / cursor:** CJKV and East Asian mathematical terminology research; program and field-policy cursor at their 2026-07-18 versions.
- **Choice:** build a typed evidence graph and declared family/cohort tree for terminology transmission and provenance. Preserve Chinese, Japanese, Korean, Vietnamese/Hán–Nôm, and later inventory branches as distinct systems. Require trap-sense windows, Mandarin-Simplified dominance debt, and basin membership (Sino-xenic inheritance / modern loan / native coinage).
- **Alternatives considered or rejected:** a pan-CJK interlanguage is rejected; shared script or learned vocabulary does not establish shared grammar. Scalar readiness, automatic terminology voting, and community-certification language are rejected. Kanbun, hanmun, and Hán–Nôm remain evidence overlays, not voting languages.
- **Evidence and hashes:** research program `04_comparison_web/CJKV_AND_EAST_ASIAN_MATHEMATICAL_CROSSWALK_RESEARCH_PROGRAM_20260718.md`, SHA-256 `1F00A8F2FEEF8D3D6DF6C370A7602AB6AE951302CD6E684A679906C08ADEE280`; field policy `04_comparison_web/CJKV_CROSSWALK_FIELD_POLICY_20260718.md`, SHA-256 `EDACDCFDBE4859CB833B2E8D8C0DFA7106C2D905A516ADF39A2BC0B4041A9350`.
- **Motivation:** capture the real shared Sino-xenic/transmission layer without erasing unrelated grammars, regional registers, or uneven corpus power.
- **Uncertainty / adverse evidence:** Vietnamese/Hán–Nôm and minority/historical branches remain thin; no comprehension trial or community validation exists; Mandarin and Simplified-Chinese corpus dominance can distort apparent consensus.
- **Consequences:** crosswalk entries are evidence-bearing mappings, not invented common forms. Historical scripts remain inventory nodes until technical evidence supports stronger use.
- **Changed artifacts:** the two cited research-control files and crosswalk schemas; no language form is certified by this decision.
- **Supersession / review state:** active; supplements CJK-D004 and CJK-D011. Requires external specialists and reader testing before stronger claims.
- **Next cursor / revisit:** first fill Vietnamese/Hán–Nôm evidence; then bounded Manchu–Mongolian–Tibetan inventory audit; revisit viability only after branch-balanced evidence and comprehension testing.
- **Claim typing:** **source fact** — cited program/policy contents; **computation** — none; **editorial inference** — crosswalk architecture and dominance safeguards; **model preference** — preference for typed edges over scalar aggregation; **external/human validation** — Fable advisory endorsement only, not community certification.

### CJK-KO-P27-004 — correct the final equivalence clause after independent internal review

- **Recorded at:** 2026-07-18T16:52:28+02:00. **Decision-time precision:** 2026-07-18 only; retrospective record, exact earlier time not captured.
- **Classification:** German wording is a source fact; the mismatch diagnosis and Korean repair are internal model review/editorial inference; no human validation.
- **Work unit / authority / cursor:** final sentence of P27, German `das Äquivalent für ... Darstellung`.
- **Choice:** replace the draft's weakened `표현에 대응하는 대상을 이룬다` with `표현에 상응하는 대체물을 이룬다`.
- **Alternatives considered or rejected:** `표현의 동등물을 이룬다` was considered as more literal but rejected as less idiomatic; retaining the original draft was rejected because it weakened the equivalent/substitute relation.
- **Evidence:** independent internal Korean audit against the complete German control identified this as the only substantive fidelity issue. Final Korean TeX SHA-256 is `7B67FFBE9A350E3CD06730A807ABF5AF4C4712B095E36983911611DBE79D8B73`; extracted PDF text contains the corrected clause.
- **Motivation:** preserve Noether's assertion that the composition series and Hilbert numbers provide the functional equivalent of the unavailable prime-power representation.
- **Uncertainty / adverse evidence:** the selected Korean phrase remains an internal editorial solution, not a native-domain certification; a human Korean algebraist may prefer a different equivalent rendering.
- **Consequences:** Korean TeX was rebuilt twice, rerendered, re-extracted, and visually reinspected. Downstream hashes were regenerated.
